Turns the operator's raw request, usually voice dictation run through speech-to-text, into a clean, agent-ready brief split into independent parts, without doing the task. Use at the start of any multi-part or dictated request, when the operator says "intake", "engineer this prompt", "clean this up and plan it", or when a request is long, garbled by STT, or mixes several asks. Not for short single-step requests, and never a substitute for doing the work once the brief is approved. ([c10vis-poem/aesop-xi](https://github.com/c10vis-poem/aesop-xi/tree/HEAD/skills/intake))
- **intake**: Generic front door for any new need, before designing anything: triage it (frontend/UI, backend analysis, bug, ticket, engineering management), look cheaply at what exists, interview the user in the main session, verify every load-bearing claim with evidence, reproduce or test when the result can change the design, then design behind an approval gate. Use when a request opens new work, or asks to fix or act on what a ticket, bug report, spec or link describes: its claims are unverified until checked. Triggers: new need, new topic, analyse ce besoin, cadrer, cadrage, nouveau sujet, nouveau tic… ([cdfarkas/cdfarkas-skills](https://github.com/cdfarkas/cdfarkas-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/intake))
